The 
Banking 
system is a way to upgrade the max currency a player can hold and gives the option to hold them in a digital bank to prevent loss. It can be accessed from the GeneratorCraft Store, using /bank ( 
#bank
 ) or using the Bankphone

Message Edited in 
#daily-rewards
 
[Jump to Message](https://discordapp.com/channels/1339343877743579297/1350586212142026893/1350586402022227992)
Before
Daily Rewards
 Every 24 hours at 00:00 the daily reward is reset. GeneratorCraft Level 5 is required to access it. Can be accessed using /daily ( 
#daily
 )
after
Daily Rewards
 Every 24 hours at 00:00 the daily reward is reset. GeneratorCraft Level 5 is required to access it. Can be accessed using /daily ( 
#daily
 ) The Daily Rewards consist of: Coins: 
`(Random[1-200])*(GC_LEVEL)`
 Gems: 
`(Random[1-100])*(GC_LEVEL)`
 One random GeneratorCraft item (any item) One Slots spin

Before
RNG Miner
 RNG Miner is a Pickaxe enchantment that can go up to tier 3 (iii). It may be obtained though various loottables, villager trades and the enchantment table If the block broken is an ore, a random chance of 
`1/(Random[1-(50,000-EnchLevel*10000])`
 is rolled. If the roll is successful, a random amount (0 to 64) of goodies will drop. Money Pouch Gem Fortunite Ingot Diamond Iron Ingot Gold Ingot Slots Token
after
RNG Miner
 RNG Miner is a Pickaxe enchantment that can go up to tier 3 (iii). It may be obtained though various loottables, villager trades and the enchantment table If the block broken is an ore, a random chance of 
`1/(Random[1-(50,000-EnchLevel*10000])`
 is rolled. If the roll is successful, a random amount (0 to 64) of goodies will drop. The Enchantment conflicts with Silk Touch and Fortune Money Pouch Gem Fortunite Ingot Diamond Iron Ingot Gold Ingot Slots Token

Message Edited in 
#slots-gambling
 
[Jump to Message](https://discordapp.com/channels/1339343877743579297/1350594172981219482/1350596067787407462)
Before
The 
Slot Machine
 is a feature in GeneratorCraft that allows you to gamble using Slot Tokens. The three slots in the GUI will roll a random item, if all three slots match the same item, the item is dropped as a reward. The loot has a forgiving system. Every spin adds 1 to the forgive counter. When the forgive counter surpasses 75 the table used to define the 3 items becomes much more empty and fair. When the counter reaches 200 it resets. The unforgiving table can have any GeneratorCraft item. The forgiving tables make all 3 slots include only: 
- Lootbox related items

after
The 
Slot Machine
 is a feature in GeneratorCraft that allows you to gamble using Slot Tokens. The three slots in the GUI will roll a random item, if all three slots match the same item, the item is dropped as a reward. The loot has a forgiving system. Every spin adds 1 to the forgive counter. When the forgive counter surpasses 75 the table used to define the 3 items becomes much more empty and fair. When the counter reaches 200 it resets. The unforgiving table can have any GeneratorCraft item. The forgiving tables make all 3 slots include only: 
- Lootbox related items
- Weapon related items
- Generator related items
- Generator Upgrades related items
- Currency related items

The 
Banking 
system is a way to upgrade the max currency a player can hold and gives the option to hold them in a digital bank to prevent loss. It can be accessed from the GeneratorCraft Store, using /bank ( 
#bank
 ) or using the Bankphone

Message Edited in 
#generatorcraft-store
 
[Jump to Message](https://discordapp.com/channels/1339343877743579297/1350607276808999042/1350607942570737745)
Before
GeneratorCraft Store
 The GeneratorCraft Store can be accessed using /shop ( 
#shop
 ) or by pressing the default keybind of B (can be changed in minecraft control settings) In the store GUI there are several buttons and upgrades: 
- Coin drop chance upgrade
- Box drop chance upgrade [TOGGLE] (toggles whether you want to drop boxes or not)
- Coin multiplier upgrade
- Gem drop chance upgrade
- Combat Log time upgrade
- Price Multiplier upgrade
Adblock (remove advertisements #advertisements )
- Remove Watermark ( #watermark )
- Gem Shop
- Slot Machine ( #slots-gambling )
- Banking ( #banking )

after
GeneratorCraft Store
 The GeneratorCraft Store can be accessed using /shop ( 
#shop
 ) or by pressing the default keybind of B (can be changed in minecraft control settings) In the store GUI there are several buttons and upgrades: 
- Coin drop chance upgrade
- Box drop chance upgrade [TOGGLE] (toggles whether you want to drop boxes or not)
- Coin multiplier upgrade
- Gem drop chance upgrade
- Combat Log time upgrade
- Price Multiplier upgrade
Adblock (remove advertisements #advertisements )
- Remove Watermark ( #watermark )
- Gem Shop ( #gem-shop )
- Slot Machine ( #slots-gambling )
- Banking ( #banking )

Gem Armor and Tools
 The Gem Armor and Tools pack is a set of Armor and Tools that can be bought from the Gem Shop ( 
#gem-shop
 ) They can be repaired in an anvil using gems The armor is about between Iron and Diamond level The Tools are slightly better than diamond
after
Gem Armor and Tools
 The Gem Armor and Tools pack is a set of Armor and Tools that can be bought from the Gem Shop ( 
#gem-shop
 ) They can be repaired in an anvil using gems The armor is about between Iron and Diamond level The Tools are slightly better than diamond They may be upgraded to Fortunite ( 
#fortunite
 ) variants

Message Edited in 
#gemstone-obelisk
 
[Jump to Message](https://discordapp.com/channels/1339343877743579297/1350616459398615162/1350617127270350888)
Before
Gemstone Obelisk
 The Gemstone Obelisk is a flex trophy block that can be bought from the Gem Shop ( 
#gem-shop
 ) After placing it down, right-clicking it will set an owner to it. Right-Clicking an obelisk that already has an owner will show in chat who own's it. Breaking an obelisk that doesn't belong to you will return it to it's owner. While holding an obelisk, could be converted back to Gems using 
`/convertobelisk`
 ( 
#convertobelisk
 )
after
Gemstone Obelisk
 The Gemstone Obelisk is a flex trophy block that can be bought from the Gem Shop ( 
#gem-shop
 ) As of GeneratorCraft 1.2.4, the Obelisk's price does not get reduced by the Price Multiplier After placing it down, right-clicking it will set an owner to it. Right-Clicking an obelisk that already has an owner will show in chat who own's it. Breaking an obelisk that doesn't belong to you will return it to it's owner. While holding an obelisk, could be converted back to Gems using 
`/convertobelisk`
 ( 
#convertobelisk
 )

Ultra Anvil
 The Ultra Anvil is a workstation that can be bough from the Gem Shop ( 
#gem-shop
 ) and can be used to combine any enchantment to any item, bypassin and limits withing minecraft's vanilla anvil (and costing gems instead of xp). It can also be used as a villager station for the Elite Enchantsmith

Before
Gambling Dice
 Gambling Dice is an item that rolls a number between 1-6 for a price. It can be obtained from various lootboxes If the numbers 1, 2, 3, 4 or 5 are rolled, nothing happens. If the numbers 6 is rolled, a reward is given. A tier 1 dice costs 2,000 coins to roll and rewards 25,000 coins A tier 2 dice costs 800 gems to rolls and rewards 2,000 gems
after
Gambling Dice
 Gambling Dice is an item that rolls a number between 1-6 for a price. It can be obtained from various loottables If the numbers 1, 2, 3, 4 or 5 are rolled, nothing happens. If the numbers 6 is rolled, a reward is given. A tier 1 dice costs 2,000 coins to roll and rewards 25,000 coins A tier 2 dice costs 800 gems to rolls and rewards 2,000 gems

Before
Weapon Lootbox
 A Weapon Lootbox is a special type of lootbox you encounter, can be obtained from loottables and opening lootboxes. The table is as such: 
- Techno Blade (Weight: 1)
- Sword of Luck (Weight: 1)
- Might of a Thousand Worlds (Weight: 10)
- Dagger of Madness (Weight: 33)
- Blade of Pure Red (Weight: 25)
- Axe of Judgement (Weight: 50)
- Axe of Midas (Weight: 45)
- Worn out Axe (Weight: 40)
- FrikandelBroodje Speciaal (Weight: 1)

after
Weapon Lootbox
 A Weapon Lootbox is a special type of lootbox you encounter, can be obtained from loottables and opening lootboxes. The table is as such: 
- #techno-blade  (Weight: 1)
- #sword-of-luck  (Weight: 1)
- #might-of-a-thousand-worlds  (Weight: 10)
- #dagger-of-madness  (Weight: 33)
- #blade-of-pure-red  (Weight: 25)
- #axe-of-judgement  (Weight: 50)
- #midas-axe  (Weight: 45)
- #worn-out-axe  (Weight: 40)
- #frikandelbroodje-speciaal  (Weight: 1)

/gamerule duperLimits [true/false]
 While true: the Duper block ( 
#duper
) may not dupe these items: 
- Big Gem ( #big-gem )
- Dragon Egg
- Duper ( #duper )
- Shulker Box
- Generators

after

 While true: the Duper block ( 
#duper
) may not dupe these items: 
- Big Gem ( #big-gem )

- Shulker Box
- Generators ( #generator-core )

Server Rules
 
1️⃣ Be Respectful
 
![🚫](https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@latest/assets/svg/1f6ab.svg)
 No harassment, hate speech, or discrimination. Treat everyone with kindness. 
2️⃣ Keep It Safe & Friendly
 
![🔞](https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@latest/assets/svg/1f51e.svg)
 No NSFW, excessive swearing, or inappropriate content. 
3️⃣ No Spam or Flooding
 
![💬](https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@latest/assets/svg/1f4ac.svg)
 Don’t spam messages, emojis, or excessive pings. 
4️⃣ Stay On Topic
 
![🗣](https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@latest/assets/svg/1f5e3.svg)
️ Discussion is the only speaking channel—keep conversations relevant and follow the channel's purpose. 
5️⃣ No Self-Promotion or Advertising
 
![📢](https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@latest/assets/svg/1f4e2.svg)
 Keep promotions, invites, and advertisements outside of this server. 
6️⃣ Follow Discord's Terms of Service
 
![📜](https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/twitter/twemoji@latest/assets/svg/1f4dc.svg)
 Anything that violates Discord's 
[Terms of Service](https://discord.com/terms)
 or 
[Community Guidelines](https://discord.com/guidelines)
 is not allowed.

Before
Weapon Lootbox
 A Weapon Lootbox is a special type of lootbox you encounter, can be obtained from loottables and opening lootboxes. The table is as such: 
- #techno-blade  (Weight: 1)
- #sword-of-luck  (Weight: 1)
- #might-of-a-thousand-worlds  (Weight: 10)
- #dagger-of-madness  (Weight: 33)
- #blade-of-pure-red  (Weight: 25)
- #axe-of-judgement  (Weight: 50)
- #midas-axe  (Weight: 45)
- #worn-out-axe  (Weight: 40)
- #frikandelbroodje-speciaal  (Weight: 1)
- The Gambler's Edge (Weight: 5)

#worldbank-economy
 
[Jump to Message](https://discordapp.com/channels/1339343877743579297/1365458224257433600/1365460244439765134)
Before
The World Bank
 The World Bank is a feature added in the 1.2.6 update of GeneratorCraft. 
- Every world has a default world bank amount of 1,000,000
- The World Bank only deals in Coins

The state of the World Bank's currency affect the Economy Boost and the Economy State:
- <-500,000 = Economy Boost: 0.1x, Economy State: Collapsed
- <0 = Economy Boost: 0.25x, Economy State: Debt Crisis
- <200,000 = Economy Boost: 0.5x, Economy State: Depression
- <500,000 = Economy Boost: 0.75x, Economy State: Recession
- <1,000,000 = Economy Boost: 1x, Economy State: Stable
- <2,000,000 = Economy Boost: 1.25x, Economy State: Booming
- <5,000,000 = Economy Boost: 1.5x, Economy State: Overheated
- <10,000,000 = Economy Boost: 2x, Economy State: Inflated
- >9,999,999 = Economy Boost: 3x, Economy State: Hyperinflation

If the Economy Boost stat is above 1x, it will only affect the prices of things (ex. upgrades, shop items)

If the Economy Boost is below 1x, it will only affect the output of things ...
after
The World Bank
 The World Bank is a feature added in the 1.2.6 update of GeneratorCraft. 
- Every world has a default world bank amount of 1,000,000
- The World Bank only deals in Coins

The state of the World Bank's currency affect the Economy Boost and the Economy State:
- <-500,000 = Economy Boost: 0.1x, Economy State: Collapsed
- <0 = Economy Boost: 0.25x, Economy State: Debt Crisis
- <200,000 = Economy Boost: 0.5x, Economy State: Depression
- <500,000 = Economy Boost: 0.75x, Economy State: Recession
- <1,000,000 = Economy Boost: 1x, Economy State: Stable
- <2,000,000 = Economy Boost: 1.25x, Economy State: Booming
- <5,000,000 = Economy Boost: 1.5x, Economy State: Overheated
- <10,000,000 = Economy Boost: 2x, Economy State: Inflated
- >9,999,999 = Economy Boost: 3x, Economy State: Hyperinflation

If the Economy Boost stat is above 1x, it will only affect the prices of things (ex. upgrades, shop items)

If the Economy Boost is below 1x, it will only affect the output of things ...

Before
@everyone
 1.2.6 has just been released and will be available to download shortly. This is probably one of the biggest updates since 1.1.5 
Changes:
- Some numbers should now display commas (",") if too big
- Main Menu, Background Dirt, and Menu Panorama have been changed to fit GeneratorCraft's aesthetic
- Added some buttons to the main menu
- Added custom Splash Texts to the main menu
- Coins and Gems will now display less than 1 coin (e.g., 5 coins → 5.25 coins)
- Events Rework: All events now run under the same clock instead of separately

- Every 50 minutes, a random event will take place for 25 minutes

- New Event: EmptiX Trade-in Event

- Upon eating food or drinking potions, a waste item will be dropped

- Waste items may be exchanged with the generous EmptiX corporation for Deposit Tokens

- Deposit Tokens may be used in the EmptiX Store while the event is active

- *(N...

@everyone
 1.2.6 has just been released and will be available to download shortly. This is probably one of the biggest updates since 1.1.5 
Changes:
- Some numbers should now display commas (",") if too big
- Main Menu, Background Dirt, and Menu Panorama have been changed to fit GeneratorCraft's aesthetic
- Added some buttons to the main menu
- Added custom Splash Texts to the main menu
- Coins and Gems will now display less than 1 coin (e.g., 5 coins → 5.25 coins)
- Events Rework: All events now run under the same clock instead of separately

- Every 50 minutes, a random event will take place for 25 minutes

- New Event: EmptiX Trade-in Event

- Upon eating food or drinking potions, a waste item will be dropped

- Waste items may be exchanged with the generous EmptiX corporation for Deposit Tokens

- Deposit Tokens may be used in the EmptiX Store while the event is active

Mythic Lootbox
 A Mythic Lootbox is the sixth type of lootbox you encounter, can be obtained from loottables and opening lootboxes. The table is as such: 
- Weapon Lootbox (Weight: 10)
- Dante Lootbox (Weight: 1)
- Generator Core (Weight: 40)
- Diamond (Weight: 15)
- Iron Ingot (Weight: 1)

![Author icon](https://images-ext-1.discordapp.net/external/ojvXv2K_I3vOS9qzRxElMjdfR9qrAh7ouOCWIdAyq8k/%3Fsize%3D4096/https/cdn.discordapp.com/avatars/498513173150695428/226c6b35e53a55c3da1eff3a20ccb7b5.png)
igndante
Message Edited in 
#mob-killing
 
[Jump to Message](https://discordapp.com/channels/1339343877743579297/1350575131034124369/1350580700700737557)
Before
After 
killing a mob
, Coins, Gems and Lootboxes may drop according to the player's respective chances The formulas are as such: Coin Drop amount from non monster entities = 
`((MaxHP/2)+Random[1-10])*CoinMultiplier`
 Coin Drop amount from monster entities = 
`(((MaxHP/2)+Random[1-10)+(MaxHP/2))*CoinMultiplier`
 Gem Drop amount from non monster entities = 
`((MaxHP/5)+Random[1-5])*GemMultiplier`
 Gem Drop amount from monster entities = 
`(((MaxHP/5)+Random[1-10)+(MaxHP/5))*GemMultiplier`
 If the 
Looting 
enchantment is present this amount is added(+) at the end of the formula: Coins = 
`CoinDrop*((1.5*LootingLevel)+(Random[1-10])`
 Gems = 
`GemDrop*((1*LootingLevel)+(Random[1-10])`
 Lootbox Drop: Lootboxes will drop according to your Box Chance. If the weapon has Looting there exists a formula to drop another Lootbox: if 
`Random[LootingLevel-10]`
 is bigger than 5
after
After 
killing a mob
, Coins, Gems and Lootboxes may drop according to the player's respective chances The formulas are as such: Coin Drop amount from non monster entities = 
`((MaxHP/2)+Random[1-10])*CoinMultiplier`
 Coin Drop amount from monster entities = 
`(((MaxHP/2)+Random[1-10)+(MaxHP/2))*CoinMultiplier`
 Gem Drop amount from non monster entities = 
`((MaxHP/5)+Random[1-5])*GemMultiplier`
 Gem Drop amount from monster entities = 
`(((MaxHP/5)+Random[1-10)+(MaxHP/5))*GemMultiplier`
 If the 
Looting 
enchantment is present this amount is added(+) at the end of the formula: Coins = 
`CoinDrop*((1.5*LootingLevel)+(Random[1-10])`
 Gems = 
`GemDrop*((1*LootingLevel)+(Random[1-10])`
 Lootbox Drop: Lootboxes will drop according to your Box Chance. If the weapon has Looting there exists a formula to drop another Lootbox: if 
`Random[LootingLevel-10]`
 => 5

Message Edited in 
#mob-killing
 
[Jump to Message](https://discordapp.com/channels/1339343877743579297/1350575131034124369/1350580700700737557)
Before
After 
killing a mob
, Coins, Gems and Lootboxes may drop according to the player's respective chances The formulas are as such: Coin Drop amount from non monster entities = 
`((MaxHP/2)+Random[1-10])*CoinMultiplier`
 Coin Drop amount from monster entities = 
`(((MaxHP/2)+Random[1-10)+(MaxHP/2))*CoinMultiplier`
 Gem Drop amount from non monster entities = 
`((MaxHP/5)+Random[1-5])*GemMultiplier`
 Gem Drop amount from monster entities = 
`(((MaxHP/5)+Random[1-10)+(MaxHP/5))*GemMultiplier`
 If the 
Looting 
enchantment is present this amount is added(+) at the end of the formula: Coins = 
`CoinDrop*((1.5*LootingLevel)+(Random[1-10])`
 Gems = 
`GemDrop*((1*LootingLevel)+(Random[1-10])`
 Lootbox Drop: Lootboxes will drop according to your Box Chance. If the weapon has Looting there exists a formula to drop another Lootbox: if 
`Random[LootingLevel-10]`
 => 5
after
After 
killing a mob
, Coins, Gems and Lootboxes may drop according to the player's respective chances The formulas are as such: Coin Drop amount from non monster entities = 
`((MaxHP/2)+Random[1-10])*CoinMultiplier`
 Coin Drop amount from monster entities = 
`(((MaxHP/2)+Random[1-10)+(MaxHP/2))*CoinMultiplier`
 Gem Drop amount from non monster entities = 
`((MaxHP/5)+Random[1-5])*GemMultiplier`
 Gem Drop amount from monster entities = 
`(((MaxHP/5)+Random[1-10)+(MaxHP/5))*GemMultiplier`
 If the 
Looting 
enchantment is present this amount is added(+) at the end of the formula: Coins = 
`CoinDrop*((1.5*LootingLevel)+(Random[1-10])`
 Gems = 
`GemDrop*((1*LootingLevel)+(Random[1-10])`
 Lootbox Drop: Lootboxes will drop according to your Box Chance. If the weapon has Looting there exists a formula to drop another Lootbox: if 
`Random[LootingLevel-10]`
 >= 5
